SHE CAME DOWN THAT SLOPE AT THE END OF `c = 28` WITH THE FRAME UNDER HER ARM AND THE BAG IN HER HAND. NO REASON IS PRINTED AS HAVING GONE OUT OF HER MOUTH ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.**

**13. THE CLERK. THIRTY-FOUR, FIFTEEN YEARS AS CLERK. **SHE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 30`. SHE READ THAT MORNING OUT LOUD BEFORE THE PEN WAS NEAR ANY PART OF IT ON EVERY MORNING ON WHICH SHE WROTE AT ALL, ON ALL TEN OF THESE. **SHE HAS A FORM OF WORDS FOR A MORNING A REASON WENT OUT LOUD AND A FORM OF WORDS FOR A MORNING NOTHING WENT OUT LOUD AND SHE HAS NEVER HAD WORDS FOR THE THIRD KIND, WHERE A FIGURE GOES OUT AND NOBODY ANSWERS IT AND THE PAGE DOES NOT MOVE.** SHE HAS A QUESTION SHE HAS NOT ASKED AND IS NOT GOING TO ASK, AND IT IS A QUESTION, AND A QUESTION IS THE ONE SHAPE OF SENTENCE THAT DOES NOT GET PUT DOWN IN THAT YARD AND WALKED AWAY FROM. **THERE IS A WORN PLACE IN THAT PAPER ABOUT FOUR INCHES IN FROM THE FOOT OF THE PAGE AND EVERY LINE SHE HAS EVER WRITTEN ACROSS IT SITS A LITTLE LOWER AND A LITTLE FURTHER RIGHT THAN THE LINES ABOVE IT, AND SHE HAS NEVER TRIED TO CORRECT FOR IT.** SHE LOOKS AT THE LINES ON THAT BACK PAGE WITHOUT THE PEN NEAR IT **PERHAPS ONCE IN TEN MORNINGS**, AND HAS NEVER TOLD ANYBODY THAT SHE DOES IT. ON `c = 22` SHE TOOK ONE LINE AND IT WENT OVER THAT WORN PLACE AND DID NOT SIT LEVEL WITH THE LINES ABOVE IT AND SHE LEFT IT WHERE IT WAS.

**14. NOBODY NEW ENTERED THAT YARD IN VOLUME 22 BLOCK 0003 AND NO NEW NAME WAS SETTLED IN IT. **`Orren Pike` IS THE ONLY NAME THE PROSE OF THIS BLOCK CARRIES, AND IT IS A NAME ALREADY ON THOSE PAGES. THE HEADING OVER THE ONE NAME STILL SAYS *WHOEVER*, THE COLUMN HOLDS ONE NAME, THE SECOND SPACE ON THE FORM IS EMPTY, AND NOBODY IS EVER ASKED TO FILL EITHER SPACE, INCLUDING BY NAMING IT.**

**15. THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT WHO KEEPS A TALLY. UNNAMED. **HE PUT HIS NUMBER INTO ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF AIR AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE ON EVERY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK AND ABOUT FOUR VOICES TOOK IT UP AFTER HIM OUT OF STEP WITH ONE ANOTHER, AND THE COUNT AT THE FOOT OF THAT BANK WAS ENTERED AFTER HE HAD SAID IT. **HIS FIGURE HAS NO INTERCEPT AND NO RULE AND WAS NOT CHECKED AND WAS NOT JOINED TO ANYBODY'S, AND NO FIGURE IS PUBLISHED ANYWHERE FOR WHAT WENT OUT INTO THAT AIR ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK OR FOR HOW MANY MORNINGS THAT WAS.**

## VOLUME 22, BLOCK 0004 — CHARACTER STATE AFTER CHAPTERS 1041 TO 1050, `c = 31` TO `c = 40`, THE LAST TEN MORNINGS

*Re-derived from Chapters 1041 to 1050 themselves. Nothing in this section is written off the day-map cards.*

**1. THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT. UNNAMED. **HE SAID ONE FIGURE OF HIS OWN OUT LOUD, ONCE, IN THE ORDINARY VOICE, ON `c = 32` ABOUT TWO MINUTES AFTER ONE OF HERS WENT OUT, ON `c = 35` AT ABOUT A QUARTER TO TWELVE ON A MORNING WHEN SHE SAID NOTHING AT ALL, AND ON `c = 40` INSIDE A MINUTE AFTER ONE OF HERS WENT OUT, ABOUT FOUR FEET AWAY, IN ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE'S HEARING. **HE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD AT ALL ON `c = 31`, `c = 34` AND `c = 37`. HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 35`. HE IS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN MORNINGS AND WAS ASKED NOTHING BEHIND THEM TOO.** ON `c = 35` HE STARTED AT THE NEAR END OF HIS OWN TABLE INSTEAD OF THE NORTH END, WHICH HE HAS DONE PERHAPS TWICE IN NINE YEARS, AND HE DID THE LOW PLACE IN HIS OWN TOP WITH HIS BARE HAND AT ABOUT HALF PAST THREE BECAUSE A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR NAMED THAT PLACE OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD AND WAS TOLD NOT TO LAY ANYTHING IN IT. THE TURNER TOLD HIM AT ABOUT ONE THAT HE WAS COMING BACK TO THE CORNER WITH THE CLOTH IN THE OTHER HAND AND HE PUT IT BACK THE WAY IT GOES. ON `c = 39` HE CAME BACK ALONG HIS OWN TOP AND DID NOT MOVE, AND HE HAS NOT GONE BACK OVER THAT HALF MINUTE IN ANY MORNING SINCE. **HIS MORNINGS IN THAT YARD ARE NOT MEASURED AND MAY NOT BE MEASURED IN ANY FORM, AND HIS BEING HEARD IS NOT A CHANGE IN ANYTHING HE IS OWED.** HE STILL HAS NO BED, NO WAGE AND NO OFFICE AND DID NOT ASK FOR ANY OF THE THREE.

**2. THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS A SCALE. UNNAMED. **SHE SAID ONE FIGURE OF HER OWN OUT LOUD, ONCE, AT HER OWN SCALE, ON `c = 32` AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE, ON `c = 39` AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE IN ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE'S HEARING, AND ON `c = 40` AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE IN ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE'S HEARING. **SHE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD AT ALL ON `c = 35`, AND SHE WAS AT HER OWN SCALE ABOUT FOUR FEET OFF THE NEAR END OF THAT TABLE THE WHOLE OF THAT MORNING.** ON NONE OF HER MORNINGS WAS SHE ANSWERED, THANKED OR ASKED WHAT SHE WEIGHS THINGS FOR, AND ON NONE DID THE PAGE TAKE A LINE, AND NOBODY ELSE ASKED HER FOR A FIGURE SHE SAYS. **SHE IS TOLD FROM ON NONE OF THE TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK.** ON `c = 40` SHE KNOTS THE CLOTH OVER HER SCALE AND PUTS THE BASKET ON HER ARM AND STANDS AT ABOUT FOUR FEET OFF THE NEAR END OF THAT SECOND TABLE WHILE ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE GO UP THAT BANK WITHOUT HER. **NOT ONE CHARACTER ABOUT HER OWN BOARD APPEARS IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND NEITHER A PIECE OF CHALK NOR A BOARD APPEARS EITHER, AND NOBODY WENT NEAR IT, COUNTED IT OR READ IT.**

**3. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR. UNNAMED. **HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 32`. **ON `c = 31` HE PUT HIS OWN TABLE UP ABOUT FOUR FEET ALONG FROM THE SECOND TABLE AND SQUARED THAT CORNER WITH THE BACK OF HIS HEEL AND WENT ALONG THE TOP OF IT WITH THE CLOTH AND SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD AT ANY POINT OF THE DAY.** HE HAD A PIECE OF PALLET WITH ONE SOUND END IN HIS COAT POCKET, GIVEN TO HIM BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO ON `c = 28`, AND HE DID NOT LAY IT UNDER THAT CORNER. **ON `c = 32` HE FOUND AT HALF PAST EIGHT THAT A THUMBNAIL OF THE CORNER HAD GONE OUT AGAIN OVERNIGHT, TOOK THE PALLET OUT, AND PUT IT DOWN FLAT ON TOP OF HIS OWN BOARDS WHERE HE CAN SEE IT AND WHERE NOBODY PUTS A BOOT. IT IS STILL THERE ON `c = 37` AND ON `c = 40`, AND HE HAS LAID IT NOWHERE.** HE TOLD THE BOY THAT THAT PALLET ON TOP OF THE BOARDS IS FOR HAVING IT WHERE HE CAN SEE IT, HE TOLD THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO THAT ABOUT HALF OF THAT GROUND IS OUT OF SQUARE AND THE OTHER HALF IS OUT OF SQUARE IN A DIFFERENT DIRECTION, HE TOLD THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT THAT HE WOULD WANT TO KNOW WHAT THE PALLET IS FOR AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO BE ABLE TO SAY, AND HE TOLD THE BOY THAT HE HAS NOTICED THE BOY IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THAT YARD WHO HAS NOT ASKED HIM A QUESTION AND THAT IS AS FAR AS HE GOES. **HE SAID NO REASON OUT LOUD ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.**

**4. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO. UNNAMED. **HE PUT HIS OWN TABLE UP AT ABOUT HALF PAST EIGHT ON `c = 31` AND SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD THAT DAY, WHICH `outline/volume-22.md` §12 GIVES HIM. HE IS THE MAN WHO STOPS ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT'S NEAR CORNER AND DOES NOT COME THE LAST FOUR FEET WHEN HE DOES NOT WANT TO BE FOUR FEET OFF A PERSON WHO IS STANDING STILL, ON `c = 38`. HE CAME AND READ THE BACK OF THE LOT BOOK UPSIDE DOWN AT ABOUT FOUR ON `c = 33` AND SAID SIX HUNDRED OUT LOUD TO THE CLERK, AND SAID THAT HE HAD BEEN AT THAT TABLE EVERY ONE OF THE DAYS IT TOOK TO GET THERE AND THAT NOBODY LOOKED UP WHEN IT CAME. **HE IS NOT TO BE MERGED WITH THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT, WHO SET NO TABLE UP AT HALF PAST EIGHT.** HIS OWN THREE HUNDRED AND SOMEBODY ELSE'S THREE HUNDRED ARE NOT THE SAME THREE HUNDRED AND WERE NOT PUT IN ONE SENTENCE.

**5. ORREN PIKE, SEVENTEEN, THE BOY. **HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 31` AND `c = 37`. HE SAID THE LANE COUNT OUT LOUD AT THOSE BOARDS BEFORE ANYBODY'S HAND MOVED ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK, AND SAID OUT LOULD THAT NOTHING WAS BEHIND IT. HE COUNTS THE NINE SPACES BETWEEN THOSE BOARDS FOUR TIMES ON ONE MORNING AND GETS NINE EVERY TIME AND CANNOT MAKE ONE THING OF IT. ON `c = 31` HE GOT DOWN ON ONE KNEE AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE AND FOUND OUT THAT THE GROUND UNDER THAT CORNER HAS GONE AND THAT NOTHING IS LAID UNDER IT, AND HE DID NOT TELL ANYBODY BECAUSE HE HAD FOUND IT OUT HIMSELF. **ON `c = 37` HE HAD A QUESTION ABOUT A FIGURE AND ABOUT NOTHING AT ALL AND DID NOT ASK IT, AND HE STOPPED ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE NEAR END OF THAT FIRST TABLE, FURTHER THAN HE CAME THE DAY BEFORE.** ON `c = 32` HE ASKED WHAT THE PALLET ON TOP OF THE BOARDS IS FOR AND WAS TOLD THE WHOLE OF THE ANSWER. HE SPOKE TO THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT TWICE ON `c = 37` AND NEITHER LINE WAS A QUESTION PUT TO HIM. **THE FIGURE HE SAYS HAS NO INTERCEPT, NO RULE AND NO CELL, WAS NOT CHECKED, IS NOT PUBLISHED, AND THE NUMBER OF MORNINGS HE HAS SAID ONE IS NOT PUBLISHED.**

**6. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FIVE WHO DUGS LOAM. UNNAMED. **HE IS IN NONE OF THE TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK AND SAID NO REASON OUT LOUD ON ANY OF THEM. HE MAY NOT ASK HER for anything and he may not ask him for anything.

**7. THE TURNER, ABOUT FORTY-ONE. **HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 40`, THE LAST MORNING OF THE VOLUME. **HE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK AND DID NOT PUT A FIGURE AND A LINE IN ONE MOUTH A SECOND TIME.** HE HAS BEEN IN THAT YARD THIRTY-ONE YEARS AND HAS A SENTENCE HE HAS NEVER GOT TO THE END OF, AND ON THE LAST MORNING HE PUT HIS HEEL ON THE FRAME AND STOPPED THE WHEEL, WHICH IS THE ONLY WAY THAT WHEEL HAS EVER BEEN STOPPED AND WHICH HE HAS NOT DONE FOR ANYTHING EXCEPT THE DAY BEING FINISHED WITH. HE PUT THE WHEEL BACK UP AT THE SAME SPEED AND DID NOT STOP IT AGAIN THAT DAY. HE TOLD THE BOY THAT THERE IS A THING HE COULD SAY ABOUT A WHEEL AND THEN SAID NO. HE CAME THROUGH THE GAP ON `c = 31`, ON `c = 32` AND ON `c = 37`, AND ON `c = 33` HE CAME AND STOPPED ABOUT FOUR FEET OFF THE CLERK AND SAID THAT IT WAS NOT HIS BUSINESS WHAT SHE DID WITH A PEN, WHICH IS THE CLOSEST HE HAS COME TO HER ONE SENTENCE IN FIFTEEN YEARS.

**8. THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE. UNNAMED. **HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 39` AND **HE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.** HE SPENT HIS ONE THING ON `c = 8` BEHIND AND IT IS NOT REOPENED AND HIS FIFTH LINE IS NOT TO BE PAID AND NOBODY MAY ASK HIM FOR IT. **ON `c = 39` HE WENT TO THE WHEELHOUSE AT ABOUT TEN AND TOOK THE MALLET OFF THE SHELF BEHIND THE DOOR THAT IS NOT SHUT, AND HE HAD BEEN TELLING HIMSELF THAT GOING TO THAT SHED MEANS PUTTING SOMETHING OUT LOUD INTO THAT YARD FIRST, AND HE HAD BEEN WRONG ABOUT THAT FOR A RUN OF MORNINGS.** HE PACKED THE NEAR LEG OF THAT BENCH WITH A FRESH STRIP OFF THE BALLE TIE AND IT DID NOT KNOCK, HE PUT HIS WEIGHT ON IT TWICE TO BE SURE, AND HE HAS NEVER TOUCHED IT AGAIN. HE TOLD THE BOY THAT A BENCH IS NOT THE SAME THING AS A PLACE. HE STOOD THERE WHEN HER FIGURE WENT OUT AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE AND SAID NOTHING ABOUT IT.

**9. THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR. UNNAMED. **HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 38`, AND **NOBODY PUT A QUESTION TO HIM ON ANY OF THE TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK**, WHICH IS THE ONE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-FIRST TO THE ONE HUNDRED AND NINETIETH MORNING RUNNING OF THAT, THE BOY INCLUDED. **HE CAME DOWN TO THAT WALL BEFORE HALF PAST SEVEN ON `c = 38`, EARLIER THAN ANY MORNING BEFORE IT, BECAUSE THE FIRST FIGURE COMES AT A QUARTER TO EIGHT AND HE WANTED THE WHOLE OF IT; HE TOLD THE TURER THAT HE HAD COME DOWN EARLY THREE TIMES AND NOT ONCE IN THE WHOLE OF THAT RUN BEFORE THE FIRST ONE, AND HE TOLD HIM THAT WITHOUT BEING ASKED.** HE USED TO READ MARKS OFF A BOARD FOR A LIVING, A TIDE BOARD AND THEN A DEPTH BOARD ON A STRETCH OF WATER THAT HAS A DIFFERENT NAME NOW, AND HE NEVER ASKS ANYBODY ANYTHING. **HE CANNOT HEAR THE CLERK FROM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AND HAS WORKED OUT THAT HE CAN TELL HOW LONG A SENTENCE IS FROM THE SHAPE OF HER MOUTH.** HE SAID NOTHING AT ALL ON THE RECORD ABOUT THE FIGURES THAT WENT OUT OF THAT YARD, AND NO FIGURE IS PRINTED FOR WHAT HE HAS PUT INTO THAT AIR OR FOR HOW LONG HE HAS PUT NOTHING.

**10. THE MAN OF SIXTY-ONE ON THE STONES. UNNAMED. **HE SAT ON THOSE STONES EVERY AFTERNOON OF THIS BLOCK AND GAVE THAT AFTERNOON ONE WORD. A MAN STANDING IN THE STRIP OF LIGHT SAID GOOD AFTERNOON TO HIM AND GOT ONE WORD BACK AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID ANYTHING ELSE ABOUT IT. HE IS FOUR FEET OFF THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR AND HEARS HIM EVERY MORNING AND HAS NEVER ONCE PUT A QUESTION TO HIM.

**11. THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX. UNNAMED. **HE GOT ALL FOUR FIGURES OFF THAT WALL TWICE A MORNING ON EVERY ONE OF THE TEN MORNINGS AND SAID HIS OWN FLAT ANSWER OUT LOUD TO THAT STONE AT ABOUT FOUR ON EVERY ONE OF THEM, **AND EVERY ONE OF THOSE TEN IS A DIFFERENT SENTENCE AND NONE OF THEM REPEATS ANY OTHER.** THE FIRST READING AT ABOUT A QUARTER TO EIGHT IS GIVEN ON EVERY ONE OF THE TEN AND THE FIGURE IS PRINTED ONCE PER FILE, AT ABOUT FOUR. **HE WAS GIVEN NO SPAN IN YEARS, DAYS, MONTHS OR FORTNIGHTS ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK AND IS MEASURED IN MORNINGS AND IN *SINCE BEFORE THE MIDDLE OF THE WINTER* AND IN NOTHING ELSE. HE WAS ASKED NOTHING OUT LOUD ON ANY OF THE TEN**, AND ON `c = 31` A BOY OF SEVENTEEN STOOD FOUR FEET OFF HIM AT ABOUT TEN AND GOT FOUR FIGURES AND NO ANSWER, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE WAS NOT GOING TO BE ASKED. **ON `c = 34` HE READ THE FOUR FROM THE BOTTOM UP AT ABOUT FOUR, WHICH HE HAS NEVER DONE, AND GOT ALL FOUR.**

**12. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO. UNNAMED. **SHE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 36`, WHICH IS THE FOURTH MORNING IN A ROW SHE HAS SPENT AT THE TOP OF THAT BANK. **SHE IS ONE OF ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE THERE WHO HAVE SAID SINCE THAT A FIGURE WENT OUT INTO THAT YARD AND HAVE NOT SAID WHAT IT WAS, AND ABOUT FOUR OF THEM HAVE NOT SAID WHAT ANYBODY OUGHT TO DO ABOUT IT.** THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR CAME UP THE BANK AND SAID THE FIRST FOUR WORDS OF THE SENTENCE SHE HAS BEEN UNABLE TO FINISH IN THREE MORNINGS AND FOUR WORDS FURTHER THAN HE GOT ON THE MORNING BEFORE, AND SAID THEY WERE NOT THE SAME FOUR AS HERS AND NOT A DIFFERENT FOUR. SHE CAME DOWN THE BANK ON `c = 36` AT ABOUT HALF PAST TWO FOR FOUR FEET OF SUN AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT THE SPACE ON THAT FORM HAS BEEN BLANK FOR LONGER THAN SHE WILL PUT A FIGURE ON, AND DID NOT FILL IT IN ON THE WAY BACK UP. SHE GAVE THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR THAT PIECE OF PALLET BACK IN THE LOOK OF IT BY TELLING THE BOY WHERE IT CAME FROM AND WHICH END WAS WRONG, ON `c = 31`, AND ON `c = 39` SHE STOOD OVER IT ON THE BOARDS AND SAID NOTHING FURTHER. **NO REASON IS PRINTED AS HAVING GONE OUT OF HER MOUTH ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.**

**13. THE CLERK. THIRTY-FOUR, FIFTEEN YEARS AS CLERK. **SHE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 33`, THE CLIMAX MORNING. SHE READ THAT MORNING OUT LOUD BEFORE THE PEN WAS NEAR ANY PART OF IT ON EVERY MORNING ON WHICH SHE WROTE AT ALL, ON ALL TEN. **SHE HAS A FORM OF WORDS FOR A MORNING A REASON WENT OUT LOUD AND ONE FOR A MORNING NOTHING WENT OUT LOUD AND SHE HAS NEVER HAD WORDS FOR THE THIRD KIND, WHERE A FIGURE GOES OUT AND NOBODY ANSWERS IT AND THE PAGE DOES NOT MOVE; SHE TOLD THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO ON `c = 33` THAT SHE HAS BEEN GETTING BY FOR ABOUT A FORTNIGHT ON THE SECOND OF THOSE AND THAT THIS MORNING IT DID NOT BE NOTHING.** THERE IS A WORN PLACE IN THAT PAPER ABOUT FOUR INCHES IN FROM THE FOOT OF THE PAGE AND EVERY LINE SHE HAS EVER WRITTEN ACROSS IT SITS A LOWER AND FURTHER RIGHT THAN THE LINES ABOVE IT, AND **SHE DID NOT COUNT THE LINES ON THAT BACK PAGE AT THE END OF `c = 33`, WHICH SHE HAS DONE PERHAPS ONCE IN TEN MORNINGS FOR FIFTEEN YEARS, AND SHE KNOWS EXACTLY WHERE SHE DID NOT COUNT THEM.** **ON `c = 38` SHE READ THE MORNING OUT LOUD AS *THE THREE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-SECOND MORNING* AND THEN WROTE *THE TWO HUNDRED AND THIRTY-SECOND MORNING* UNDER THE LAST LINE IN HER OWN HAND, AND BOTH FIGURES ARE ON THE PAGE AND NEITHER IS HARMONISED WITH THE OTHER.** SHE IS NEVER ASKED WHY SHE SAYS A MORNING OUT LOUD BEFORE SHE WRITES IT, IS NEVER ASKED TO SAY IT AGAIN, IS NEVER ASKED WHAT A LINE IS FOR, AND NO PERSON RESPONDS TO ANYTHING SHE SAYS.

**14. THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT WHO KEEPS A TALLY. UNNAMED. **HE PUT HIS NUMBER INTO ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF AIR AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE ON EVERY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK AND ABOUT FOUR VOICES TOOK IT UP AFTER HIM OUT OF STEP WITH ONE ANOTHER, AND THE COUNT AT THE FOOT OF THAT BANK WAS ENTERED AFTER HE HAD SAID IT. **HIS FIGURE HAS NO INTERCEPT AND NO RULE AND WAS NOT CHECKED AND WAS NOT JOINED TO ANYBODY'S, AND NO FIGURE IS PUBLISHED ANYWHERE FOR WHAT WENT OUT INTO THAT AIR ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK OR FOR HOW MANY MORNINGS THAT WAS. NO CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK REPORTS HIM AS SILENT AND NONE MAY.**

**15. NOBODY NEW ENTERED THAT YARD IN VOLUME 22 BLOCK 0004 AND NO NEW NAME WAS SETTLED IN IT. **`Orren Pike` IS THE ONLY NAME THE PROSE OF THIS BLOCK CARRIES, AND IT IS A NAME ALREADY ON THOSE PAGES. THE HEADING OVER THE ONE NAME STILL SAYS *WHOEVER*, THE COLUMN HOLDS ONE NAME, THE SECOND SPACE ON THE FORM IS EMPTY, AND NOBODY IS EVER ASKED TO FILL EITHER SPACE, INCLUDING BY NAMING IT.

## VOLUME 23, BLOCK 0001 — CHARACTER STATE AFTER CHAPTERS 1051 TO 1060, `c = 1` TO `c = 10`, THE OPENING TEN MORNINGS

*Re-derived from Chapters 1051 to 1060 themselves. Nothing in this section is written off the day-map cards.*

**1. THE TURNER, ABOUT FORTY-ONE. UNNAMED. HE IS THE REASON THIS VOLUME EXISTS.** HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 1` AND `c = 3`. **HE TOOK A LENGTH OF BELTING OFF A NAIL IN THE WHEELHOUSE ON `c = 1`, PUT IT IN HIS COAT, LOOKED AT THE FRAME WITH IT IN HIS HAND, AND PUT IT BACK IN HIS COAT, AND THE LENGTH IS STILL IN HIS COAT ON `c = 3`, AND HE DOES NOT FIT IT ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.** HE BANKED THE WHEEL BY SIX AND A HALF EVERY MORNING OF THE BLOCK AND IT WAS AT ITS SPEED BY TEN TO EIGHT ON ALL TEN. **ON `c = 3` HE PUT HIS HEEL ON THE FRAME AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE AND THE WHEEL CAME OFF IT, AND HE STOOD WITH HIS HAND ON THE FRAME LOOKING AT THE BOARDS, AND HE TOOK THE HEEL OFF AND THE WHEEL TOOK UP EXACTLY THE SPEED IT HAD BEEN GOING AT, AND HE DID NOT STOP IT AGAIN THAT DAY.** **HE GOT AS FAR AS THE FRONT OF HIS SENTENCE ON `c = 1` AND ON `c = 3` AND STOPPED, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD KNOWS HE GETS THAT FAR AND HE HAS NOT TOLD ANYBODY.** **HE WAS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN MORNINGS.** HE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD ABOUT THE FRAME OR THE WHEEL ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK; HIS ONLY LINES IN IT ARE ABOUT WOOD AND ABOUT THE BOARDS GOING UP AT HALF PAST FOUR. HE PUTS HIS HAND ON THE FRAME AT ABOUT HALF PAST FOUR EVERY MORNING TO STOP IT AT THE END OF THAT DAY, WHICH IS THE ORDINARY ACT AND IS PRINTED AS SUCH. **HE WAS GIVEN NO SPAN IN YEARS, DAYS, MONTHS OR FORTNIGHTS; HIS THIRTY-ONE YEARS IS PRINTED AS A PLAIN CONSTANT AND WAS NOT TURNED INTO A COUNT OF MORNINGS, AND IT WAS NOT USED AS A SPAN FOR ANY OTHER THING IN HIS HANDS.** HE STILL HAS NO BED, NO WAGE, NO OFFICE AND NO NAME.

**2. THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT. UNNAMED.** HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 9`. **HE IS AT ABOUT FOUR FEET OFF THE NEAR END OF THAT FIRST TABLE FROM ABOUT HALF PAST NINE UNTIL ABOUT FOUR ON ALL TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK, AND HE SAID NOTHING OUT LOUD AT ALL ON ALL TEN OF THEM, AND HE WAS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN.** ON `c = 9` HE FOUND, BY PUTTING HIS HEEL ON THE BOARDS, THAT THE GROUND UNDER THE NEAR END OF HIS OWN TABLE HAS GONE DOWN ABOUT THE WIDTH OF TWO FINGERS, THAT THE TABLE ROCKS AT THAT END, THAT HE CANNOT LIFT IT WITH THE CLOTH AND HIS BACK, CANNOT GET A HAND UNDER THE FRAME TO FIND OUT HOW FAR THE GROUND HAS GONE, CANNOT LIFT THE TOP OFF IT ALONE, AND CANNOT ASK ANYBODY TO HELP HIM. **HE CHANGED THE WAY HE WORKS: SHORT LENGTHS OF ABOUT TWO FEET INSTEAD OF LONG ONES, BECAUSE THE MIDDLE OF THAT TABLE IS OVER SOUND GROUND, AND A LONG LENGTH STILL TOOK HIM FOUR GOES AND LEFT A SMEAR HE HAD TO GO BACK OVER.** **HIS MORNINGS IN THAT YARD ARE NOT MEASURED AND MAY NOT BE MEASURED IN ANY FORM, AND HIS BEING HEARD IS NOT A CHANGE IN ANYTHING HE IS OWED.** HE STILL HAS NO BED, NO WAGE AND NO OFFICE AND DID NOT ASK FOR ANY OF THE THREE. HE DID NOT GO UP THAT LANE IN THIS BLOCK.

**3. THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS A SCALE. UNNAMED.** SHE SET UP ABOUT FOUR FEET OFF THE NEAR END OF THAT SECOND TABLE AT ABOUT HALF PAST TWELVE ON `c = 1` TO `c = 3` AND AT ABOUT HALF PAST TWELVE ON THE OTHER MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK AS IT FELL TO HER, AND SHE PUT A BAR ON HER OWN PAN AND LET IT WANT TO FOLLOW THE WIND UNTIL IT CAME STILL. **SHE SAID A FIGURE OF HER OWN OUT LOUD ON NONE OF THE TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK, AND SHE WAS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN.** **SHE IS TOLD FROM ON NONE OF THE TEN.** **NOT ONE CHARACTER ABOUT HER OWN BOARD APPEARS IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND NEITHER A PIECE OF CHALK NOR A BOARD APPEARS AS HERS IN ANY OF THEM; THE CHALK IN THESE FILES IS THE EDGE OF THAT SECOND TABLE AND NOTHING ELSE, AND SHE IS NEVER IN THE SAME SENTENCE AS A PIECE OF CHALK.**

**4. THE CLERK. THIRTY-FOUR, FIFTEEN YEARS AS CLERK. SHE IS ONE WOMAN AND NOT TWO.** SHE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 10`. **SHE READ THAT MORNING OUT LOUD AT ABOUT TEN PAST FOUR ON ALL TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK, WITH THE PEN STILL IN THE HOLDER AND BEFORE SHE HAD HER HAND NEAR ANY PART OF IT, AND SHE WROTE THE MORNING UNDER THE LAST LINE ON THAT BACK PAGE IN HER OWN SMALL CAREFUL HAND AND TOOK NO LINE FOR IT ON ALL TEN.** ON `c = 10` SHE DID THE THING SHE DOES PERHAPS ONCE IN TEN MORNINGS, WHICH IS TO LOOK AT THE LINES ON THAT BACK PAGE WITHOUT THE PEN NEAR IT, AND SHE GOT SIXTY-TWO AND GOT SIXTY-TWO AGAIN FROM THE BOTTOM UP, **AND THE SECOND ONE TOOK HER THREE GOES AND SHE WAS NOT CERTAIN OF THE THIRD.** **ON `c = 10` SHE ASKED THE BOY HOW MANY SPACES THERE ARE IN THOSE BOARDS, HE SAID NINE, AND SHE DID NOT WRITE NINE ON ANYTHING AND NEVER HAS.** THERE IS A WORN PLACE IN THAT PAPER ABOUT FOUR INCHES IN FROM THE FOOT OF THE PAGE AND EVERY LINE SHE HAS EVER WRITTEN ACROSS IT SITS A LOWER AND FURTHER RIGHT THAN THE LINES ABOVE IT, AND **ON `c = 10` HER LINE WENT ACROSS IT AND SHE LEFT IT WHERE IT WAS.** SHE IS NEVER ASKED WHY SHE SAYS A MORNING OUT LOUD BEFORE SHE WRITES IT, NEVER ASKED TO SAY IT AGAIN, AND NOBODY ASKED HER WHAT A LINE IS FOR ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.

**5. ORREN PIKE, SEVENTEEN, THE BOY.** HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 8`. **HE SAID THE LANE COUNT OUT LOUD AT THOSE BOARDS BEFORE THE CLERK'S HAND MOVED ON ALL TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK, AND IT IS THIRTY ON ALL TEN, AND HE SAID OUT LOUD THAT THERE WAS NO FIGURE AFTER IT.** **HE SAID A FIGURE OF HIS OWN OUT LOUD ON NONE OF THE TEN, AND THE NINE SPACES AND THE THIRTY PAGES ARE THE ONLY TWO NUMBERS HE SAID IN ANY OF THEM.** ON `c = 8` HE SPENT THE WHOLE MORNING TRYING TO GET A NUMBER OUT OF THAT YARD THAT COMES OUT DIFFERENT TWICE IN ONE DAY AND COULD NOT: FOUR ON THE WALL, ONE BOARD THE LIGHT COMES OVER FIRST, ONE THING THROUGH THE GAP, TEN BOARDS IN THE STACK, NINE SPACES EVERY TIME, AND HE COUNTED THE STACK FROM BOTH ENDS AND CORRECTED HIMSELF FROM NINE TO TEN BECAUSE THE SHORT ONE AT THE BOTTOM IS NOT A SPACE. **HE MARKED THE BOTTOM BOARD OF THAT STACK WITH THE SIDE OF HIS PENCIL, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO TOLD HIM THE MARK IS NOT HIS TO MAKE, BECAUSE IF IT MOVES FOUR INCHES IN A MORNING THE BOARDS HAVE GONE AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD WILL KNOW HOW FAR THEY WENT, AND HE ANSWERED THAT A MARK THAT IS WRONG ON SOME MORNING IS HOW YOU FIND OUT.** **HE DECIDED, ON `c = 8`, TO GO ON SAYING THE SAME NUMBER OUT LOUD, AND THE REASON HE GIVES HIMSELF CHANGED OVER THE DAY FROM THE FACT THAT IT IS HIS WORK TO THE FACT THAT IT IS THE ONE THING IN THAT YARD HE IS SURE OF.** **HE HAS NEVER ASKED WHAT THE WHEEL IS FOR AND NOBODY HAS EVER TOLD HIM THERE IS A QUESTION THERE, AND THIS BLOCK CHANGES NEITHER FACT.** THE FIGURE HE SAYS HAS NO INTERCEPT, NO RULE AND NO CELL, WAS NOT CHECKED, IS NOT PUBLISHED, AND THE NUMBER OF MORNINGS HE HAS SAID ONE IS NOT PUBLISHED.

## VOLUME 23, BLOCK 0003 — CHARACTER STATE AFTER CHAPTERS 1071 TO 1080, `c = 21` TO `c = 30`

**1. THE TURNER, ABOUT FORTY-ONE. UNNAMED. HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 27` AND `c = 30`.** HE BANKED THE WHEEL BY SIX AND A HALF EVERY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK, **EXCEPT ON `c = 30`, WHERE IT WAS NOT AT ITS SPEED BY TEN TO EIGHT AND WAS NOT AT ITS SPEED AT ANY HOUR OF THAT DAY.** **ON `c = 27` AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE HE PUT HIS HEEL ON THAT FRAME AND THE WHEEL CAME OFF, AND HE KEPT THE HEEL THERE, AND THEN HE TOOK IT OFF AND STOOD WITH HIS HANDS AT HIS SIDES AND WENT AND DID THE NEXT THING ON THE LIST HE HAS HAD FOR THAT MORNING, AND HE DID NOT PUT THAT HEEL BACK ON THAT FRAME AGAIN THAT DAY. ON `c = 30` HE PUT IT BACK ON AT ABOUT TEN PAST FOUR, AT THE END OF THAT DAY, AFTER THE CLERK HAD READ THAT MORNING OUT LOUD, AND THE WHEEL WENT UP TO EXACTLY THE SPEED IT ALWAYS GOES AT, AND THAT IS THE ONLY THING THAT HAS EVER STARTED IT, IN THIRTY-ONE YEARS.** **HE WAS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN MORNINGS.** HE SPEAKS — TO THE BOY AT THE BOARDS AT TEN TO EIGHT, TO THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR ABOUT THAT MAN'S OWN CORNER AND ABOUT WHICH WAY THE WATER COMES OFF THAT BANK, TO THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO AT ABOUT HALF PAST TWO ON `c = 27`, AND TO THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR ABOUT A LENGTH OF BELTING AT ABOUT ONE ON `c = 30` — **AND HE IS NEVER ASKED ANYTHING.** **HE CAME UP TO THE FRONT OF HIS ONE SENTENCE ON `c = 27` AND ON `c = 30`, AT HOURS THAT ARE NOT THE HOUR HIS HEEL WAS ON THAT FRAME, HE STOPPED THERE BOTH TIMES, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD KNOWS HE GETS THAT FAR.** **ON `c = 27` HE TOOK A LENGTH OF BELTING OFF ITS NAIL AT ABOUT A QUARTER TO EIGHT, HELD IT IN THE DOORWAY OF THAT WHEELHOUSE WITH IT IN BOTH HANDS, LAID IT FLAT ON THE BOARDS AT THE NEAR END OF THAT FRAME OUT OF EVERYBODY'S BOOTS, AND STATED TO HIMSELF THAT THE REASON HE HAS NOT FITTED IT IS THAT HE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT IT GOES ON AND HE IS NOT A MAN WHO FITS A THING TO FIND OUT. ON `c = 30` HE PUT IT ALONG THE TOP RAIL OF THAT FRAME AND AGAINST THE EAVE OF THAT WHEELHOUSE, FOUND IT A FOOT TOO LONG FOR THE FRAME AND GOT NO MEASUREMENT AT THE WHEELHOUSE BECAUSE THE DOOR IS OFF ITS UPPER HINGE, DID NOT CUT IT, DID NOT FIT IT, PUT IT DOWN FLAT ON THOSE BOARDS WHERE IT HAD BEEN WITH ITS DAMP END UNDER IT, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE HAS STOPPED WORKING OUT WHAT THAT LENGTH IS FOR BECAUSE THERE IS NOBODY IN THAT YARD WHO COULD TELL HIM AND HE WOULD HAVE TO MAKE IT UP.** THE NAIL IS STILL IN THE WALL OF THAT WHEELHOUSE WITH NOTHING ON IT AND THE DOOR OF THAT WHEELHOUSE IS STILL OFF ITS UPPER HINGE. HE STILL HAS THE GREASE ON THAT CASTING AND IT IS NOT COMING OFF. **HIS THIRTY-ONE YEARS IS PRINTED AS A PLAIN CONSTANT AND WAS NOT TURNED INTO A COUNT OF MORNINGS; A DRAFT OF `c = 30` DID EXACTLY THAT AND IT WAS UNDONE.** HE STILL HAS NO BED, NO WAGE, NO OFFICE AND NO NAME.

ON `c = 23` HE SAYS NOTHING OUT LOUD AT ALL.** **ON `c = 23` HE WORKED OUT SOMETHING ABOUT HIMSELF HE HAD NOT TOLD ANYBODY AND WAS NOT GOING TO: THAT HE PULLS HIS CLOTH ON THE TURN OF THAT WHEEL AND NOT ON THE TURN OF ANYTHING ELSE IN THAT YARD, BECAUSE THE STONE COMES UP OUT OF THE GROUND AND OFF THE FACE OF THAT FRAME AND GOES DOWN BEHIND IT AND SOMEWHERE IN BETWEEN IT IS EASIER THAN ANYWHERE ELSE.** HE TRIED THE NEAR END WITH THE TURN TAKEN OUT OF IT AT ABOUT TEN AND IT CAME ABOUT FOUR INCHES AND STOPPED AND CAME ABOUT TWO INCHES MORE AND STOPPED, FOUR TIMES OVER ABOUT FORTY MINUTES, **AND THE BOARDS UNDER THAT NEAR END WENT DOWN THE WIDTH OF TWO FINGERS AND WENT DOWN FURTHER IN THE LAST HALF HOUR THAN IN THE LAST FOUR DAYS.** HE STOOD UP AND LOOKED AT THAT FRAME FOR A WHILE AND WENT BACK TO THE TURN OF IT AT ABOUT ELEVEN AND DID ABOUT SIX FEET IN A PULL, WAITING EACH TIME TO SEE WHETHER IT WOULD GO AGAIN. **THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR STOPPED, WAITED FOR A REPLY THAT DID NOT COME BECAUSE THERE WAS NOT GOING TO BE ONE, AND SAID SO OUT LOUD, AND HE SAID NOTHING BACK.** HIS MORNINGS IN THAT YARD ARE NOT MEASURED AND MAY NOT BE MEASURED IN ANY FORM. HE STILL HAS NO BED, NO WAGE AND NO OFFICE, DID NOT ASK FOR ANY OF THE THREE, AND DID NOT GO UP THAT LANE IN THIS BLOCK.

ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE HEARD IT AND EVERY ONE OF THEM WAS DOING SOMETHING ELSE AT THE TIME.** **SHE WAS ASKED NOTHING ON ALL TEN MORNINGS.** **NOT ONE CHARACTER ABOUT HER OWN BOARD APPEARS IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND NEITHER A PIECE OF CHALK NOR A BOARD APPEARS AS HERS IN ANY OF THEM; THE CHALK IN THESE FILES IS THE EDGE OF THAT SECOND TABLE AND NOTHING ELSE, IT IS IN THE READOUT ON ALL TEN MORNINGS AND OUT OF THE PROSE ON ALL TEN, AND SHE IS NEVER IN THE SAME SENTENCE AS A PIECE OF CHALK.** ON `c = 23` SHE IS FOUR FEET OFF THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT'S NEAR END FOR THE REST OF THAT DAY AND NEITHER OF THEM SAYS ONE WORD. HIS FIGURE HAS NO INTERCEPT, NO RULE AND NO CELL, WAS NOT CHECKED AND WAS NOT PUBLISHED.

ON `c = 25` HIS OWN COUNT OF THAT RUN AND THE LADDER FIGURE FOR IT ARE THE SAME FIGURE IN TWO PLACES ON THAT PAGE, EIGHT HUNDRED, WHICH IS THE HOUSE'S OWN RULE ABOUT A COUNT AND A LADDER ROW SHARING ONE FIGURE AND NOT AN ERROR.** **ON `c = 25` HE SAID HIS FOUR WORDS AT THE VOLUME HE HAS ALWAYS SAID THEM IN WITH THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR NINE FEET OFF HIM AT THAT WALL, AND STOOD THERE AND WORKED OUT HALF A SECOND LATER THAT HE HAD SAID THEM WITHOUT KNOWING ANYBODY WAS THERE BECAUSE HE HAS NEVER LOOKED, AND THAT LOOKING IS A THING YOU DO WHEN YOU EXPECT SOMETHING BACK; HE DID NOT GO BACK AND LOOK, HE WENT AND HAD HIS DINNER OUT OF THE WIND, AND AT ABOUT FOUR HE SAID OUT LOAD THAT HE IS NOT GOING BACK TO ASK AND IS NOT GOING TO SAY ONE WORD ABOUT IT AT THE TOP OF THAT BANK THAT EVENING, AND HE DID NOT.** HE STOOD AT THE TOP OF THAT BANK AT THE END OF THAT DAY AND SAID NOTHING TO ANY OF THE ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE STANDING THERE.

UNNAMED.** HIS OWN TABLE STOOD AT THAT END OF THAT GROUND THE THREE HUNDRED AND FIFTY-FOURTH TO THE THREE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-THIRD MORNING, ENTERED, AND IS NEITHER THE SECOND TABLE NOR THE NEAR RAIL. **ON `c = 27` AT ABOUT TWO HE CAME OUT FROM AMONG HIS OWN BOARDS AND SAID A REASON OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD IN THE ORDINARY VOICE, AND IT WAS THAT EVERY EIGHTH BOARD IN A STACK THAT COMES DOWN THAT SLOPE IS A DIFFERENT DEAL FROM THE OTHER SEVEN, THAT IT HAS BEEN THAT AS LONG AS HE HAS BEEN PUTTING TABLES UP, THAT HE HAS NEVER BEEN ABLE TO FIND OUT WHO CHANGED THEM OVER, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO KEEP IT TO HIMSELF ANY LONGER. **10. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO. UNNAMED. THE SECOND SPACE ON THAT FORM WAS EMPTY FOR THE WHOLE OF THAT MORNING AND NOBODY SAID A WORD TO HER ABOUT IT.** HER FOUR WORDS WERE THREE FURTHER THAN THEY HAD BEEN ON THE GROUND THE DAY BEFORE AND SHE GAVE THEM TO NOBODY ON THAT BANK AND DID NOT GIVE THEM TO A BOY OF SEVENTEEN ON THE WAY DOWN.

**11. THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE. UNNAMED, ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN, FIFTEEN YEARS AT THAT TABLE. HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 29`.** **ON `c = 29` THE NEAR END OF HIS OWN TABLE HAD BEEN LIFTING SINCE BEFORE THE MIDDLE OF THE WINTER AND THE STRIP HE PUT IN THAT RIB CAME OUT OF IT ON ITS OWN THAT MORNING, WHICH HE HAD EXPECTED AND HAD NOT WANTED.** HE LOOKED ALONG THE WHOLE FIFTEEN YEARS OF THAT ONE THING AND FOUND THAT **NOTHING HAD GONE UNDER THE MIDDLE OF THAT TABLE AND NOTHING HAD GONE UNDER THE FAR END OF IT, AND THAT IT HAD GONE UNDER THE NEAR END OF IT, AND ABOUT THE SAME AS IT HAD GONE UNDER THE NEAR END OF THE FIRST TABLE, AND IN THE SAME GROUND.** HE TOOK HIS HAND OUT AND STOOD THERE AND SAID NOT ONE WORD ABOUT IT TO ANYBODY, AND NOBODY WAS NEAR ENOUGH TO HEAR IF HE HAD. HE PUT A STRIP OF HIS OWN BACK IN THAT RIB AT ABOUT HALF PAST THREE AND IT DID NOT HOLD EITHER AND HE LEFT IT IN ANYWAY, BECAUSE THE WIND GETS UNDER A STRIP AND A STRIP KEEPS THE RAIN OUT OF A RIB FOR A WHILE. **HIS ONE THING IN THIS VOLUME IS ON `c = 37` AND IS NOT SPENT HERE, HIS FIFTH LINE IS NOT PAID, AND NOBODY MAY ASK HIM FOR IT AND NOBODY ASKED HIM ANYTHING ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK** except the boy, who asked him at ten to eight what he was going to say at that hour.

**12. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES THE CART. UNNAMED.** HE SAYS NOTHING OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD AND **NO MORNING OF THIS BLOCK GIVES HIM A LINE, AND NO MORNING OF THIS BLOCK PUTS HIM IN THAT YARD AT ALL.**

**13. THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT WHO KEEPS A TALLY. UNNAMED.** HE PUT HIS NUMBER INTO ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF AIR AT ABOUT HALF PAST ONE ON **ALL TEN** MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK, IN THE VOICE HE KEEPS FOR IT, AND ABOUT FOUR VOICES TOOK IT UP AFTER HIM OUT OF STEP WITH ONE ANOTHER. **HIS FIGURE HAS NO INTERCEPT, NO RULE AND NO CELL, WAS NOT CHECKED AND WAS NOT JOINED TO ANYBODY'S, AND NO FIGURE IS PUBLISHED ANYWHERE FOR WHAT WENT OUT INTO THAT AIR OR FOR HOW MANY MORNINGS THAT WAS. NO CHAPTER OF THIS BLOCK REPORTS HIM AS SILENT AND NONE MAY.**

**14. THE MAN OF SIXTY-ONE ON THE STONES. UNNAMED. HE IS TOLD FROM ON `c = 21`.** HE CAME UP THAT BANK AT ABOUT EIGHT AND STOOD AT THE END OF IT BECAUSE THERE IS NOWHERE AT THE TOP OF IT TO SIT AND LOOKED AT ELEVEN FEET OF BOARDS WITH THE LIGHT LYING FLAT ALONG THEM. **HE HAS BEEN COMING UP THAT BANK IN THE MORNING FOR SEVENTEEN YEARS AND HAS COME UP IT IN THE AFTERNOON THREE HUNDRED TIMES, AND THE TWO WERE NOT THE SAME WALK AND HE HAD NEVER TOLD ANYBODY THAT.** HE SAT ON THOSE STONES FOR **THE THREE HUNDREDTH** AFTERNOON RUNNING TO THE THREE HUNDRED AND NINTH, ENTERED, AND ONE WORD IS THE WHOLE OF EACH OF THOSE AFTERNOONS. **ON `c = 21` THE LIGHT STAYED ON THOSE STONES ABOUT TWENTY MINUTES LONGER THAN IT EVER HAS, AND THE WORD CAME OUT AT ABOUT TWENTY PAST FOUR INSTEAD OF TEN, AND HE HAD KNOWN FOR THREE HUNDRED AFTERNOONS THAT A WET STONE THAT HAS DRIED HOLDS THE LIGHT AND HAD NEVER HAD IT HAPPEN AT THAT HOUR.** HE IS FOUR FEET OFF THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR AND HEARS HIM EVERY MORNING AND HAS NEVER ONCE PUT A QUESTION TO HIM, AND ON `c = 21` THAT MAN DID NOT TURN ROUND. **HE IS NOT ONE OF THE ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE AT THE TOP OF THAT BANK AND NOT ONE OF THE FOUR HAS EVER ASKED HIM WHAT THE WORD IS.** **HIS FIGURE AND THE COUNT OF COUNTED MONTHS ARE NEVER PUT IN THE SAME SENTENCE IN ANY OF THESE TEN FILES.**

**15. NOBODY NEW ENTERED THAT YARD IN VOLUME 23 BLOCK 0003 AND NO NEW NAME WAS SETTLED IN IT.** `Orren Pike` IS THE ONLY NAME THE PROSE OF THIS BLOCK CARRIES, AND IT IS A NAME ALREADY ON THOSE PAGES. THE HEADING OVER THE ONE NAME STILL SAYS *WHOEVER*, THE COLUMN HOLDS ONE NAME, THE SECOND SPACE ON THE FORM IS EMPTY ON ALL TEN MORNINGS, AND NOBODY IS EVER ASKED TO FILL EITHER SPACE, INCLUDING BY NAMING IT.
